### [122] [Closure-channel identifiability and two-channel recovery in monatomic kinetic normal shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12447)
*Ehsan Roohi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了动理学/矩方程中残余一致性无法自动识别非平衡激波中所有高阶闭合变量的问题，将其形式化为四阶闭合内容的可观测性问题，并通过碰撞模型和诊断层级进行验证。运动学部分独立于碰撞算子：一维热流预算仅观测到投影四阶通道S=R^cl_xx+Δ/3，而非独立的R26级矩R^cl_xx和标量四阶过剩Δ。观测映射具有一维零空间，热流残差可能很小但张量各向异性与各向同性尾部强度之间的分裂仍然错误。DVM一致的标量过剩预算提供了缺失通道，实现无需直接R^cl_xx数据的双通道重构。在Mach 2-5的BGK激波中，该方法将活跃区R^cl_xx误差从63-64%降至2.4-4.1%。稀疏标量过剩插值作为信息缩减测试，24探针操作点R^cl_xx误差低于4.5%，1%探针噪声下低于4.7%。碰撞模型诊断将不变观测通道与模型依赖的源定律分离。Shakhov模型改变热流弛豫至正确Prandtl数，但在偶次|c|^4标量过剩源上是中性的；直接离散Shakhov通道检查恢复S、Δ和R^cl_xx的误差分别为6.4×10^{-4}、2.1×10^{-7}和1.0×10^{-3}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动理学/矩方程中的残余一致性（如热流残差）无法自动确保所有高阶闭合变量的正确性，导致非平衡激波模拟中高阶矩（如四阶矩R^cl_xx和标量过剩Δ）可能同时错误而无法被检测。本文旨在形式化和解决这一可观测性问题。

Method: 将四阶闭合内容的可观测性问题形式化，通过运动学分析识别观测映射的零空间（一维），提出DVM一致的标量过剩预算作为补充观测通道，实现双通道重构。通过BGK和Shakhov碰撞模型的数值实验验证，包括稀疏探针插值测试和噪声鲁棒性分析。

Result: 运动学部分独立于碰撞算子，观测映射存在一维零空间；标量过剩预算可恢复缺失信息，在Mach 2-5的BGK激波中将R^cl_xx误差从63-64%降至2.4-4.1%；稀疏插值（24探针）下误差低于4.5%，1%噪声下低于4.7%；Shakhov模型下直接通道检查恢复S、Δ和R^cl_xx误差极小（10^{-3}-10^{-7}量级）。

Conclusion: 热流残差不足以唯一确定所有四阶闭合变量，必须引入额外的标量过剩观测通道才能实现完整的四阶矩重构。该运动学结论普遍适用于不同碰撞模型，为高阶矩闭合的验证和校正提供了理论框架和实用方法。

Abstract: Residual agreement in a kinetic or moment equation does not automatically identify every higher-order closure variable entering a nonequilibrium shock. We formulate this issue as an observability problem for the fourth-order closure content of monatomic normal shocks and follow it through a hierarchy of collision models and diagnostics. The kinematic part of the result is independent of the collision operator: the one-dimensional heat-flux budget observes the projected fourth-order channel $S=R^{\cl}_{xx}+Δ/3$, not the tensorial R26-level moment $R^{\cl}_{xx}$ separately from the scalar fourth-order excess $Δ$. The observation map therefore has a one-dimensional null space, so a heat-flux residual can be small while the split between tensorial anisotropy and isotropic tail intensity remains wrong. A DVM-consistent scalar-excess budget supplies the missing channel and gives the two-channel reconstruction $R^{\cl}_{xx}=S-Δ/3$ without direct $R^{\cl}_{xx}$ data. Across BGK shocks at Mach 2--5, this reduces the active-zone $R^{\cl}_{xx}$ error from about $63$--$64\%$ to $2.4$--$4.1\%$. Sparse scalar-excess interpolation is used only as an information-reduction test: a representative 24-probe operating point gives $R^{\cl}_{xx}$ errors below $4.5\%$, and below $4.7\%$ with $1\%$ probe noise. Collision-model diagnostics then separate the invariant observation channel from the model-dependent source law. Shakhov changes the heat-flux relaxation to the correct Prandtl number but is neutral in the even $|\boldsymbol c|^4$ scalar-excess source; a direct discrete Shakhov channel check recovers $S$, $Δ$ and $R^{\cl}_{xx}$ with errors $6.4\times10^{-4}$, $2.1\times10^{-7}$ and $1.0\times10^{-3}$, respectively.

</details>


### [123] [Black Hole Polarimetry III: Universal Polarization of Synchrotron Radiation at the Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12518)
*Andrew Chael,Alexandru Lupsasca,George N. Wong,Zachary Gelles,Eliot Quataert*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞事件视界附近同步辐射的线性偏振模式，发现该模式具有普适性——仅由黑洞自旋和观测者倾角决定，与磁场几何无关。通过解析公式和GRMHD模拟验证，提出未来微角秒分辨率VLBI观测可探测M87*的视界偏振趋势，从而测量黑洞自旋并验证Blandford-Znajek能量提取过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定近事件视界电磁能流是由吸积等离子体提供还是由黑洞自旋提供，即验证Blandford-Znajek过程是否主导能量提取。

Method: 在时间稳态、轴对称、退化的克尔磁层中，考虑视界贯穿场线基部的同步辐射线性偏振，推导出依赖于黑洞自旋和观测者倾角的解析偏振公式，并与广义相对论磁流体动力学（GRMHD）模拟的时间平均图像进行对比验证。

Result: 发现观测到的偏振模式具有普适性：完全由黑洞自旋和观测者倾角决定，与磁场几何无关，且该模式在GRMHD模拟的时间平均图像中也得到趋近。

Conclusion: 未来微角秒分辨率的VLBI观测可通过探测M87*趋近视界偏振值的趋势，实现黑洞自旋的新测量，并为视界贯穿磁场线通过Blandford-Znajek过程提取自旋能量提供证据。

Abstract: Polarized images of a black hole encode the direction of electromagnetic energy flow near its event horizon. Measuring polarization from near-horizon emission can determine whether this energy flow is powered by the accreting plasma or the black hole spin. Here we consider the linear polarization of synchrotron radiation emitted from the base of horizon-threading field lines in a time-stationary, axisymmetric, and degenerate Kerr magnetosphere. We show that the observed polarization pattern displays universal behavior: it is completely determined by the black hole spin and observer inclination and is independent of the magnetic field geometry. We derive a simple analytic formula for this spin-dependent horizon polarization pattern. We find that this predicted pattern is also approached in time-averaged images from General Relativistic Magnetohydrodynamic simulations. Future observations with Very-Long-Baseline Interferometry at microarcsecond resolution could detect the trend of polarization toward the unique horizon value in M87*. Such observations may enable new measurements of black hole spin and provide evidence that magnetic field lines thread the horizon and extract spin energy via the Blandford--Znajek process.

</details>


### [124] [Implementation of multi-grid Poisson solver in numerical relativity and its application to gravitational collapse of massive star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12542)
*Kenta Kiuchi,Hirotada Okawa*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种新的基于网格的多重网格泊松求解器，应用于数值相对论领域。通过双黑洞、静态中子星、均匀旋转中子星和引力坍缩大质量恒星等初始值问题测试了其性能，并在9倍太阳质量恒星的引力坍缩模拟中成功应用，展示了良好的守恒精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值相对论中的引力模拟需要高效精确的泊松求解器来处理约束方程，现有方法在分辨率和计算效率方面存在不足，亟需开发新的多重网格求解器以支持更复杂的引力坍缩模拟。

Method: 开发了一种基于网格的多重网格泊松求解器，采用约束保持的网格重划分策略，在多个测试案例（双黑洞、静态/旋转中子星、引力坍缩大质量恒星）中验证性能，并在9倍太阳质量恒星的数值相对论中微子辐射传输流体动力学模拟中实际应用至核反弹阶段。

Result: 在9倍太阳质量恒星的引力坍缩模拟中，重子质量、ADM质量和ADM类角动量的守恒精度分别达到O(10^{-3})%和O(10^{-2})–O(10^{-1})%。

Conclusion: 新开发的多重网格泊松求解器在数值相对论的多类初始值问题中表现良好，能够在引力坍缩模拟中有效保持约束条件，实现高精度的质量与角动量守恒。

Abstract: We develop a new grid-based multi-grid Poisson solver in numerical relativity. We report the performance of the multi-grid Poisson solver in the initial value problems for two-puncture black holes, a static spherical neutron star, a uniformly rotating neutron star in equilibrium, and a gravitationally collapsing massive star. As a demonstration, we conduct a numerical-relativity neutrino-radiation-transfer hydrodynamics simulation of the gravitational collapse of the $9M_\odot$ massive star in Ref.~\cite{Aguilera-Dena:2020mfh} up to the core bounce. During the simulation, we employ the constraint-preserving regird prescription with the newly developed multi-grid Poisson solver to improve the resolution. It shows that the baryonic mass, the Arnowit-Deser-Misner (ADM) mass, and the ADM-like angular momentum are, respectively, preserved with $O(10^{-3})\%$ and $O(10^{-2})$--$O(10^{-1})$\% accuracy.

</details>


### [125] [Spectral analysis of magnetized advective accretion flows around rotating black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12543)
*Mayank Pathak,Shubhrangshu Ghosh,Banibrata Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过数值稳态MHD解和GRMHD模拟（SANE和MAD），系统研究了黑洞自旋、吸积率、磁场和电子温度等参数对磁化吸积盘光谱的影响，发现这些参数显著改变光谱峰值和整体光度，且SANE与MAD流在辐射特征上存在巨大差异，可作为区分天体物理系统中磁场特性的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞周围吸积盘光谱是揭示吸积流及黑洞本身物理的基本诊断工具，但磁场、自旋、吸积率和温度等参数如何影响光谱尚需系统研究，尤其需要对比不同磁场构型（SANE vs MAD）下的辐射特征差异。

Method: 首先求解磁化吸积流绕黑洞的数值稳态MHD解，系统研究各参数对光谱的影响；随后利用GRMHD模拟，采用SANE和MAD两种矢量势，选取两种黑洞自旋（a=0.5和a=0.9375）建模磁场位形，对比分析所得光谱。

Result: 光谱对黑洞自旋、吸积率、磁场和电子温度表现出强烈依赖性；SANE与MAD流在辐射光度、发射峰位置以及同步辐射与同步自康普顿化峰值比等特征上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 光谱的整体光度结合同步辐射与SSC峰值比等指标，可以有效区分天体物理系统中的磁场特性（SANE vs MAD）。

Abstract: The spectra of an accretion disk around black holes are the basic diagnostic tool to enlighten the underlying flows and then black holes. Accretion flows around black holes, however, are controlled by parameters like the magnetic field, spin of the black hole, accretion rate and temperature of the flow. These quantities affect the (magneto)hydrodynamics of the flow thus consequently lead to variations in the spectrum. We first consider numerical steady state magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) solutions of magnetized accretion flows around black holes to study the dependence of the spectra on these disk properties. The spectrum exhibits strong dependence on the spin of the black hole, accretion rate, magnetic field and the electron temperature of the flow. Variations in these quantities influence the emission peaks and overall luminosity, which can be a tell-tale sign to extract physics of observed spectra. We further validate our results with general relativistic MHD (GRMHD) simulations using the standard and normal evolution (SANE) and magnetically arrested disk (MAD) vector potentials. We consider two black hole spins ($a=0.5$ and $a=0.9375$) to model the magnetic field configurations and study the resulting spectra by comparing MAD and SANE results. We find a large difference in the bolometric luminosities and the location of the emission peaks between SANE and MAD flows. Certain properties of the spectra, like, the ratio of synchrotron radiation to synchrotron self-Comptonization peaks in SANE and MAD, show drastically distinct features. The overall luminosity combined with such metrics can distinguish the magnetic field characteristics in astrophysical systems.

</details>


### [126] [Spectroscopic modeling of ionic structure in stellar winds of high-mass X-ray binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12723)
*G. Sanjurjo-Ferrín,V. Grinberg,I. El Mellah,J. M. Torrejón,R. Ballhausen,J. Planelles-Villalba,S. Reyero-Serrantes,J. J. Rodes-Roca,M. Martínez-Chicharro*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算框架，将相位分辨光谱变异性与辐照恒星风的三维结构联系起来，通过贝叶斯推断实现对大质量X射线双星系统中风辐照参数的物理约束，并成功复现了轨道相位相关的趋势与电离平衡参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量X射线双星（HMXBs）为研究强外部X射线辐照下的辐射驱动星风提供了天然实验室，但风密度和电离结构的轨道相位变化与系统几何参数的关联机制尚需系统建模，亟需一个能连接观测变异性与物理参数的推断框架。

Method: 结合光电离计算、参数化风描述和轨道几何，构建三维密度与电离图；引入团块化处理考虑几何掩星和风不均匀性；利用贝叶斯推断将模型预测与相位分辨观测数据进行对比，量化参数约束与简并性。

Result: 框架成功复现了辐照风预期的轨道相位相关趋势，对系统电离平衡给出稳健约束；风参数和光度参数组合约束良好，但个别参数仍存在部分简并性，取决于轨道构型和数据质量。

Conclusion: 该模块化、计算高效的框架为从物理角度解释HMXB相位变异性提供了可行路径，并为未来扩展至正向光谱建模和星族层面应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: High-mass X-ray binaries (HMXBs) provide a natural laboratory to study radiatively driven stellar winds under strong external X-ray irradiation. As the compact object moves along its orbit, the wind density and ionization structure vary with orbital phase, leaving characteristic signatures in X-ray emission and absorption features. The amplitude and morphology of this variability depend on the system geometry, including the orbital inclination (via line-of-sight and occultation effects) and the orbital eccentricity (via phase-dependent changes in the orbital separation). We present a computational framework that connects phase-resolved spectroscopic variability to the three-dimensional structure of irradiated winds, and that enables inference of physically meaningful wind--irradiation parameters within a Bayesian setting. We combine photoionization calculations with a parametric wind description and orbital geometry to construct three-dimensional maps of density and ionization. From these maps we compute orbital-phase-dependent diagnostics, accounting for geometric occultation and wind inhomogeneity through a clumping prescription. We then use Bayesian inference to compare model predictions with phase-resolved observables and to quantify parameter constraints and degeneracies. The framework reproduces the main orbital-phase-dependent trends expected for irradiated winds and yields robust constraints on the system ionization balance. While combinations of wind and luminosity parameters are well constrained, individual parameters can remain partially degenerate depending on the orbital configuration and data quality. This modular and computationally efficient approach provides a route to interpret HMXB phase variability in physical terms, and offers a foundation for future extensions toward forward spectral modeling and population-level applications.

</details>


### [127] [Simulations of 3-Dimensional Recoil Response to Coherent Elastic Neutrino-Nucleus Scattering Events in Directional Direct Dark Matter Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13469)
*Kwang-Chang Lai,Chung-Lin Shan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文模拟了太阳硼-8中微子引起的三维相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（CEvNS）事件，发现其核反冲通量/能量的特征环状角分布呈现出明显的年度变化，与WIMP信号的固定模式形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期研究银河系WIMP三维核反冲响应的基础上，进一步模拟太阳硼-8中微子产生的CEvNS事件，以区分中微子背景与WIMP信号。

Method: 通过数值模拟计算三维CEvNS事件，分析核反冲通量/能量的角分布及其在不同天球坐标系中的年度变化。

Result: CEvNS事件呈现特征性的环状角分布，且沿入射太阳中微子运动轨迹方向表现出明显的年度变化，而WIMP信号则呈现近似固定的模式；此外，该分布没有实验上可区分的靶核依赖性。

Conclusion: CEvNS和WIMP信号在角分布模式上的差异（环状vs固定）及其年度变化特征可用于区分中微子本底与暗物质信号。

Abstract: Following our earlier work on studying 3-dimensional nuclear recoil response to Galactic Weakly Interacting Massive Particles (WIMPs) in directional direct Dark Matter detectors, in this paper, we simulate 3-D coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CEvNS) events induced by Solar B-8 neutrinos. Our numerical results show that, in contrast to the approximately fixed patterns of the WIMP-induced signals, the characteristic ring-like angular distributions of the nuclear recoil flux/energy of CEvNS events show clearly annual variations along the trajectories of the moving direction of incident Solar neutrinos in different celestial coordinate systems without experimentally distinguishable target dependence.

</details>


### [128] [Data-driven modeling of Galactic diffuse emission with multi-wavelength observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12832)
*Xi Liu,Xiaodong Li,Sujie Lin,Yihan Liu,Chengyu Shao,Lili Yang,Le Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用多频段射电观测数据（30-857 GHz）和费米伽马射线数据（50 MeV-814 GeV），通过监督机器学习构建了从射电辐射到伽马射线强度的非线性映射。模型在0.1-10 GeV范围内实现了高预测精度（R²>0.90），识别出高频射电波段是0.1-10 GeV伽马射线强子起源的主要预测因子，低频射电波段则关联10 GeV以上轻子起源。残差图揭示了Loop I和III等大尺度结构，表明标准星际发射模型存在偏差。与GALPROP模型相比，该方法在银心区域实现了R²=0.95和14.7%的平均绝对相对误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究银河系弥散伽马射线发射与多频段射电辐射之间的物理关联，验证不同能量段伽马射线的产生机制（强子vs.轻子起源），并识别标准星际介质模型中未被充分描述的结构成分。

Method: 使用多频段Planck射电/微波图（30-857 GHz）和Fermi-LAT伽马射线数据（50 MeV-814 GeV），通过监督机器学习构建从射电辐射到伽马射线强度的非线性映射模型。

Result: 模型在0.1-10 GeV范围达到R²>0.90，高频射电波段是0.1-10 GeV伽马射线强子起源的强预测因子，低频射电关联10 GeV以上轻子起源。残差图揭示了Loop I和III等大尺度结构。与GALPROP相比，在银心区域R²=0.95，平均绝对相对误差14.7%。

Conclusion: 机器学习可作为多信使天体物理学中物理可解释的工具，提供数据驱动的基线以分离非标准发射成分，并导出关于宇宙线传播和星际介质结构的新约束。

Abstract: We present a data-driven investigation of Galactic diffuse emission. Using multi-frequency Planck radio/microwave maps (30-857 GHz) and Fermi-LAT gamma-ray data (50 MeV-814 GeV), we construct a nonlinear mapping between radio emission and gamma-ray intensity through supervised machine learning. Our models achieve high predictive accuracy (R^2 > 0.90 in the 0.1-10 GeV range), demonstrating that multi-frequency radio observations encode sufficient information to reconstruct both spatial morphology and spectral properties of diffuse gamma-ray emission. By analyzing model performance across different frequency bands and spatial regions, we identify high-frequency radio bands as the dominant predictor, providing direct empirical support for the hadronic origin of Galactic 0.1-10 GeV gamma rays, while low-frequency radio bands for the leptonic origin above 10 GeV. Residual maps reveal coherent large-scale structures, including Loop I and III, highlighting regions where standard interstellar emission models are incomplete or biased. Compared with the GALPROP model, our machine learning approach yields a higher R^2=0.95 and lower mean absolute relative error (14.7%) in the inner Galactic disk and the Galactic center region. Our results illustrate that machine learning serves as a physically interpretable tool for multi-messenger astrophysics, providing a data-driven baseline for separating non-standard emission components and deriving new constraints on cosmic-ray propagation and interstellar medium structure.

</details>


### [129] [Constraining inhomogeneities and asymmetries in SNe, FBOTs, and other high-energy transients from unresolved radio observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12846)
*Fabio De Colle,Rosa L. Becerra,Lizeth A. Meza,Nayana A. J.,James K. Leung,Luca Izzo,Raffaella Margutti,Gerardo Urrutia,Enrique Moreno-Méndez,Leonardo García-García*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从同步自吸收(SSA)谱中推断高能暂现源发射区均匀性和对称性的模型无关方法，并将其应用于SN 2016coi和AT2018cow，揭示了前者发射区的不均匀性和后者的不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能暂现源（如超新星、快速蓝色光学暂现源、潮汐瓦解事件、伽马射线暴）的射电频谱在光厚区常呈现出比标准SSA模型（$F_ν\propto ν^{5/2}$）更平坦的斜率或更宽的谱结构，现有解释多为唯象性，无法揭示发射区的结构信息。因此需要一种能够直接从SSA谱中推断发射区均匀性和对称性的方法。

Method: 通过理论分析建立SSA谱斜率与发射区均匀性、对称性之间的定量关系，讨论不同不均匀性条件下自吸收频率以下谱斜率的变化规律，并分析可约束参数与退化参数。将该方法应用于剥离包层超新星SN 2016coi和快速蓝色光学暂现源AT2018cow的观测数据。

Result: SN 2016coi的SSA谱提供了发射区存在不均匀性的强证据，AT2018cow则显示出不对称性特征。方法成功从观测中约束了发射区的结构参数。

Conclusion: 当有充分采样的射电频谱时，该方法可作为通用的、模型无关的推断工具，适用于各类未分辨的高能天体物理暂现源，包括超新星、快速蓝色光学暂现源、潮汐瓦解事件和伽马射线暴。

Abstract: Synchrotron emission in high-energy transients is produced by relativistic electrons accelerated by shocks. As high-energy transients are often unresolved even on angular scales probed by very long baseline interferometry, it is difficult to obtain a full picture of the ejecta and circumstellar medium (CSM) properties that are probed by the radio synchrotron emission. Radio spectra of high-energy transients frequently show optically thick slopes shallower than the standard $F_ν\propto ν^{5/2}$ expected from synchrotron self-absorption (SSA) models, or broader spectra near the self-absorption frequency. Such deviations are often interpreted phenomenologically, without providing clear insights into the structure of the emitting region. We show how information on the homogeneity and symmetry of the emitting region can be directly inferred from SSA spectra, even when the source is unresolved. We discuss the circumstances under which inhomogeneities in the emitting region can change the spectrum below the self-absorption frequency, causing it to follow a different slope. We examine which parameters can be constrained from observations and which remain degenerate. We apply this method to the stripped-envelope supernova (SN) 2016coi and to the fast blue optical transient (FBOT) AT2018cow, showing that SSA spectra constrain the degree of inhomogeneity in these systems, providing strong evidence for inhomogeneities in the emitting region in the SN 2016coi, and asymmetry in the case of AT2018cow, and we infer the characteristics of the emitting region. When well sampled spectra are available, our method can be applied as a general, model-independent, inference method. This approach can be used to constrain inhomogeneities in a variety of unresolved high-energy astrophysical transients, including SNe, FBOTs, tidal disruption events and gamma-ray bursts.

</details>


### [130] [Gravitational wave background from extreme-mass-ratio inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12952)
*Haoyu Zhao,Yuanhao Zhang,Xilong Fan,Wenbiao Han*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋进（EMRI）产生的引力波背景（GWB）的统计特性，发现最终偏心率分布对GWB强度影响可忽略，SMBH自旋使特征应变增强约1%，而作为致密天体的黑洞（BH）产生的信号强度比中子星（NS）或白矮星（WD）高约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI系统是LISA探测器的主要目标源，其产生的GWB是探测星系中心天体物理和动力学过程的重要工具，因此需要系统研究其统计特性和关键参数影响。

Method: 通过分析GWB的计算方法、EMRI源的天体物理分布以及关键参数（SMBH自旋、CO质量）的影响，确定EMRI产生的GWB特征应变的分布范围。

Result: （1）最终偏心率分布对GWB强度影响可忽略，因为EMRI在可探测前已快速圆化；（2）SMBH自旋使特征应变增强约1%；（3）BH作为CO时，信号强度比NS或WD高约一个数量级。

Conclusion: EMRI的GWB特征应变受CO质量影响显著，SMBH自旋影响较小，偏心率影响可忽略，这为未来LISA探测和EMRI源建模提供了重要的参数约束。

Abstract: The gravitational wave background (GWB) produced by extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) serves as a powerful tool for probing the astrophysical and dynamical processes in galactic centers. EMRI systems are a primary target for the space-based detector LISA due to their long-lived signals and high signal-to-noise ratios. This study explores the statistical properties of the GWB from EMRI, focusing on the calculation methods for the GWB, the astrophysical distribution of EMRI sources, and the influence of key parameters, including the spin of supermassive black holes (SMBHs) and the masses of compact objects (COs). By analyzing these factors, we determine the distribution range of the characteristic strain of the GWB from EMRIs. We find that the final eccentricity distributions appear to have negligible effect on the intensity of the GWB due to rapid circularization before they become detectable and the spin of the SMBH enhances the GW characteristic strain by approximately 1$\%$ compared to cases without spin effects. The masses of COs can also significantly affect the characteristic strain of the GWB from EMRIs, with Black Hole (BH) as CO producing a GW signal intensity that is approximately one order of magnitude higher compared to cases where Neutron Star (NS) or White Dwarf (WD) are the COs.

</details>


### [131] [A Delayed Multi-channel Progenitor for Apparently Nonrepeating Fast Radio Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12960)
*Zhao-Wei Du,Xi-Long Fan,Yi-Xiao Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用统计方法研究一次性快速射电暴（FRB）的红移演化，发现其本征事件率密度不直接遵循恒星形成历史（SFH），最佳拟合模型为混合模型（平均延迟时间1.426 Gyr），表明一次性FRB可能源于延迟的、多通道的前身星演化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性FRB的起源尚不明确，其红移演化特征有助于区分不同前身星模型（如直接追踪SFH、延迟模型等），从而揭示其物理起源。

Method: 采用统计种群方法，比较了纯SFH追踪模型、现象学延迟模型、物理驱动延迟模型（双中子星并合通道和中子星年龄窗口通道）以及混合模型（两个物理模型归一化后加权混合），利用CHIME/FRB目录数据进行贝叶斯推断。

Result: CHIME/FRB目录数据不支持一次性FRB的本征事件率密度直接遵循SFH。最优模型为混合模型，有效平均延迟时间 $\barτ=1.426^{+0.032}_{-0.035}~\mathrm{Gyr}$。

Conclusion: 当前数据支持一次性FRB由延迟的、可能涉及多通道的前身星演化过程所解释，而非直接追踪恒星形成。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are millisecond-duration radio flashes of unknown origin, observationally classified into repeating and apparently nonrepeating (one-off) populations. In this work, we use a statistical population approach to investigate the redshift evolution of one-off FRBs. We compare a pure star formation history (SFH) tracing model, phenomenological delayed models, physically motivated delayed models that correspond to binary neutron star related and neutron star age-window channels, and mixture models which is obtained when two physically motivated models are normalized separately and then combined as a weighted mixture. The samples in CHIME/FRB Catalog do not support an intrinsic event rate density that directly follows the SFH. The preferred model is mixture model corresponds to an effective mean delay time of $\barτ=1.426^{+0.032}_{-0.035}~\mathrm{Gyr}$. These results suggest that the current data may naturally explained by delayed, possibly multi-channel progenitor evolution for the one-off FRBs.

</details>


### [132] [Deep optical spectroscopic monitoring of the pulsating ULX NGC 1313 X-2 with longslit Gemini observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13116)
*Rajath Sathyaprakash,Timothy. P. Roberts*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用Gemini-South长缝光谱数据对ULX NGC 1313 X-2的伴星性质进行分析，发现A型超巨星可能是其供体星，并更新了轨道参数、吸积盘尺度及气体泡膨胀动力学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示ULX NGC 1313 X-2的伴星性质，理解该超亮X射线源的双星系统本质和形成演化历史。

Method: 基于2009年Gemini-South档案数据，堆叠10晚流量定标光谱，利用恒星模板拟合光谱；通过X射线与光学变化的时延-频率谱研究吸积盘尺度；利用[O III]发射线轮廓模型约束风/喷流的动能功率。

Result: 在GMOS-S光谱中检测到4000埃以下的巴耳末跳变，提示供体星为A型超巨星；更新了双星轨道参数约束；获得了吸积盘尺度及气体泡膨胀动力学信息。

Conclusion: NGC 1313 X-2的伴星很可能是A型超巨星，研究提供了更完整的双星系统参数和吸积-喷流能量分配约束，补充了多波段观测对双星形成历史的认知。

Abstract: This study reports the nature of the companion star to the pulsating ULX NGC 1313 X-2, using long-slit spectroscopic data from Gemini-South observations, based on archival data from 2009. After stacking flux-calibrated spectra from ten nights of observations and fitting the spectra with stellar templates, we find a possible Balmer break in the GMOS-S spectrum below 4000 Angstroms, which is suggestive of an A-type supergiant donor. Using the inferred stellar radii, we report updated constraints on the orbital parameters of the system and on the nature of the binary. We also add some information on the accretion disc size scale by studying the X-ray and optical variability using the lag-frequency spectrum and corroborate on results from earlier studies for the gas bubble expansion rates by modelling the [O III] emission line profiles, allowing constraints on the kinetic power of the wind/jet relative to the accretion power. This study also expands on previous efforts to study the formation history of the binary using multi-wavelength observations.

</details>


### [133] [Multi-Epoch X-Ray Detection of SLSN-I 2018bsz: Constraints on the Powering Mechanism and Ejecta Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13128)
*Julia Ahlvind,Josefin Larsson,Dennis Alp,Ragnhild Lunnan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: SN 2018bsz是迄今最近的光学剥离超亮超新星（SLSN-I），基于四期钱德拉和一期XMM的X射线光谱研究（爆炸后87-1253天）发现其X射线持续探测，成为第二颗X射线探测的SLSN-I。分析表明毫秒磁星引擎低估了观测亮度且无法解释平坦光变，而抛射物与周际介质早期相互作用更能解释观测结果，支持SN 2018bsz属于相互作用主导的SLSN-I子类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SN 2018bsz作为迄今最近的剥离型超亮超新星（SLSN-I），为探究这类极端爆发的物理机制提供了理想实验室。此前SLSN的X射线探测极为稀少，缺乏足够的观测约束来区分磁星引擎、相互作用等不同能量来源模型。

Method: 基于四期钱德拉（Chandra）X射线观测和一期XMM-Newton观测，覆盖爆炸后87至1253天的时域范围，进行多历元X射线光谱分析。通过对X射线光度曲线的时域演化建模，系统比较磁星中央引擎模型与抛射物-周际介质相互作用模型的预测结果。

Result: SN 2018bsz在所有钱德拉历元均被探测到，在晚期XMM观测中也存在疑似探测，成为第二颗X射线探测的SLSN-I和第三颗X射线探测的SLSN。磁星模型显著低估了观测到的X射线光度，且无法再现相对平坦的光变曲线；考虑抛射物吸收后模型与观测的差异进一步扩大。相比之下，早期抛射物与周际介质的相互作用模型能更好地解释平坦时域演化特征。

Conclusion: 观测结果支持SN 2018bsz属于一个独特的SLSN-I子类，其中抛射物与周际介质的相互作用对强发射起关键作用，而非单纯的磁星中央引擎主导。这意味着部分SLSN-I的能量来源机制需要重新审视，相互作用过程在其光度输出中扮演了比以往认知更重要的角色。

Abstract: SN 2018bsz is the closest known stripped superluminous supernova (SLSN-I) to date, making it an ideal laboratory for investigating the physical mechanisms powering this class of extreme explosions. We present a multi-epoch X-ray spectroscopic study of SN 2018bsz based on four Chandra observations followed by one XMM observation, spanning 87 to 1253 days after explosion. The source is detected at all Chandra epochs and is also tentatively detected in the late XMM observation, although more uncertain due to nearby contaminating sources. Regardless of the XMM detection, this makes SN 2018bsz the second X-ray detected SLSN-I and the third X-ray detected SLSN overall. We explore potential power sources for the observed X-ray emission and find that a millisecond (ms) magnetar central engine underpredict most of the observed X-ray luminosities and fail to reproduce the relatively flat light curve. Accounting for ejecta absorption further increases the discrepancy. While asymmetries and magnetar-driven ionization could reduce the effective absorption, ionization breakout is expected years after our observational window. Instead, the observations are more readily explained by early-time interaction between the ejecta and the circumstellar medium, while the magnetar emission is absorbed by the ejecta. This scenario is supported by the flat temporal evolution, previous optical results, and inferred mass-loss rates which resemble those of stripped SNe that later evolve into interacting systems. Our results thus favor the scenario where SN 2018bsz is part of a distinct group of SLSN-I, where interaction is crucial for the strong emission.

</details>


### [134] [Classification of Compact Stars via Machine Learning and Neural Network Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13369)
*D. Neraki,G. Koufetidis,I. Stergakis,Th. Diakonidis,Ch. C. Moustakidis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习和深度学习技术，基于大量中子星和夸克星的物态方程数据集，通过质量、半径和潮汐形变等可观测宏观性质，实现了对致密星内部组成（中子星vs夸克星）的高精度分类识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多信使天文学的发展虽然增进了对致密物质的理解，但致密星的内部组成（中子星、夸克星或混合星等）仍不确定，亟需一种能从宏观可观测量推断其内部成分的方法。

Method: 基于大量描述中子星和夸克星的物态方程数据集，生成对应的质量-半径关系，覆盖广泛的星体构型；采用机器学习和深度学习技术训练分类模型，通过质量、半径和潮汐形变等可观测量对致密星类型进行分类。

Result: 适当组合的可观测量能够以非常高的准确率区分中子星和夸克星，表明机器学习方法在致密物质成分探测方面具有潜力。

Conclusion: 机器学习方法可用于从宏观性质推断致密星内部组成，但未来需纳入混合星及其他奇异物质形态以验证该方法的鲁棒性和普适性。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimessenger astronomy, particularly through gravitational-wave observations of compact-object mergers, have significantly improved our understanding of dense matter. Nevertheless, the internal composition of compact stars remains uncertain. Depending on the underlying equation of state (EoS), these objects may be neutron stars composed primarily of nucleons, quark stars made of deconfined quark matter, or hybrid stars containing both hadronic and quark phases. More exotic constituents, such as hyperons, meson condensates, or dark matter, have also been proposed. In this work, we investigate whether the internal composition of compact stars can be inferred from observable quantities, including mass, radius, and tidal deformability. To address this problem, we employ machine-learning and deep-learning techniques trained on a larg dataset of EoSs describing both neutron stars and quark stars. From these EoSs, we generate the corresponding mass radius relations spanning a wide range of stellar configurations. The resulting dataset is used to train and evaluate classification models aimed at identifying the nature of compact objects from their macroscopic properties. Our results indicate that suitable combinations of observables can distinguish neutron stars from quark stars with very high accuracy. These findings demonstrate the potential of machine-learning approaches as tools for probing the composition of dense matter. However, further studies incorporating additional scenarios, including hybrid stars and other exotic forms of matter, are required to establish the robustness and general applicability of this methodology.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Estimating Individualized Treatment Effects in Acute Ischemic Stroke with Causal Transformation Models (TRAM-DAG): A Multi-Centre Observational Study with External RCT Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12623)
*Oliver Dürr,Lisa Herzog,Pascal Bühler,Susanne Wegener,Beate Sick*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出使用因果变换模型（TRAM-DAG）从观察性数据中估计急性缺血性卒中患者接受机械取栓 vs 溶栓的个体化治疗效果（ITE），并在MR CLEAN随机对照试验人群中进行验证，发现ITE估计值的均值与试验报告的ATE一致，且能正确排序患者的良好预后频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急性缺血性卒中的个性化医疗需要超越平均治疗效果（ATE），转向个体化治疗效果（ITE）估计以支持治疗决策。现有RCT仅提供群体平均效应，无法回答"哪个患者更受益于机械取栓"这一临床关键问题。

Method: 采用基于有向无环图的因果变换模型（TRAM-DAG），在MAGIC多中心观察性数据上训练（筛选NIHSS≥6的子集以匹配MR CLEAN纳入标准），然后将模型应用于MR CLEAN人群进行ITE估计，通过与ATE对比和良好预后排序进行验证。

Result: ITE估计值的均值与MR CLEAN试验报告的ATE一致；ITE估计能够正确排序试验患者的良好预后（mRS≤2）观察频率。

Conclusion: TRAM-DAG等因果模型能够从观察性数据出发，弥合观察性证据与RCT之间的差距，支持卒中治疗中的个性化决策。

Abstract: Personalized medicine in acute ischemic stroke requires moving beyond average treatment effects (ATE) to individualized treatment effect (ITE) estimates to support treatment decisions. In acute ischemic stroke, mechanical thrombectomy has been shown to be more effective on average than lysis in randomized controlled trials (RCTs), such as the MR CLEAN study. We aim to identify which individual patients benefit most from mechanical thrombectomy compared to lysis. The outcome of interest is the modified Rankin Scale (mRS) at three months, an ordinal measure of functional disability (0: no symptoms, 6: death). We demonstrate that causal transformation models on directed acyclic graphs (TRAM-DAG) can be used for ITE estimation after being fitted on observational MAGIC multi-center stroke patient data. To ensure comparability with the MR CLEAN population, which we use for validation, we train the TRAM-DAG on a MAGIC sub-population with NIHSS at admission >= 6, corresponding to one inclusion criterion of MR CLEAN. The fitted model is then used to estimate ITEs for stroke patients in the MR CLEAN population. While these ITE estimates cannot be confirmed experimentally, we show that their average is consistent with the trial's reported ATE. Furthermore, the ITE estimates correctly rank trial patients by their observed frequency of a good outcome (mRS at three months <= 2). These findings support the use of causal models like TRAM-DAG for personalized decision-making in stroke care and highlight their ability to bridge the gap between observational evidence and clinical trials.

</details>


### [136] [The Persistent Non-Response Bias in a Sample-Matched Poll for the 2024 U.S. Presidential Election](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12889)
*Jay Chooi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文运用数据缺陷相关框架分析2024年美国大选民调偏差，发现特朗普支持者的无应答偏差与2016年同级（ρ=-0.0030），哈里斯支持者在调整 turnout 后存在正向应答偏差。选前偏差校正估计量将RMSE从0.13降至0.05。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2024年大选民调再次低估特朗普支持率，重复2016年的系统性偏差，迫切需要理解偏差来源并提出校正方法。

Method: 采用数据缺陷相关框架，分析6万受访者的合作选举研究数据，运用样本匹配、人口调整及基于历史数据缺陷相关系数和 turnout 率的选前偏差校正估计量。

Result: 特朗普选民无应答偏差持续存在（ρ=-0.0030 vs 2016年-0.0045），哈里斯选民存在正向应答偏差；民调误差与州人口规模正相关，最大州有效样本量缩减超99%；偏差校正估计量将RMSE从0.13降至0.05。

Conclusion: 选前偏差可基于历史数据进行有效校正，效果优于传统选后加权方法，为未来民调改进提供新路径。

Abstract: Donald Trump won the 2024 US Presidential Election despite polls predicting a Democratic lead, echoing the polling miss in 2016. Using the data defect correlation framework, we revisit the 60,000-respondent Cooperative Election Study and find that non-response bias for Trump voters persists on the same order of magnitude ($ρ=-0.0030$ vs $-0.0045$ in 2016) even under sample-matching to the US adult population. We additionally find evidence of positive response bias for Harris voters after adjusting for turnout. Consistent with findings in 2016, polling errors scale with state population size, and larger samples produce greater departures from conventional confidence intervals, with reductions of effective sample size exceeding 99% in the largest states. We propose a pre-election bias correction estimator informed by historical data defect correlations and turnout rates that decreases RMSE from 0.13 to 0.05 using only prior election data, comparable to post-election weighting (RMSE 0.09).

</details>


### [137] [Stochastic Modeling of Composite Interfaces: Sensitivity to Spatial Correlation and Bayesian Identification from Standard Fracture Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13019)
*Elton Donfack-Siewe,Sylvain Dubreuil,Christian Fagiano,Jérôme Morio,Jean-Philippe Navarro*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机有限元框架，通过空间相关随机场表征层合板界面变异性，结合近似贝叶斯计算方法从实验数据提取关键参数（相关长度），以提升航空航天复合材料（特别是加筋脱粘）的可靠性评估与虚拟测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合材料结构中的不确定性难以数值化处理，现有方法无法充分考虑界面变异性对脱粘失效的影响，亟需一种能够将散射效应纳入更高尺度仿真与测试的方法，以提升航空航天复合材料结构的可靠性评估。

Method: 采用随机有限元框架，利用空间相关随机场表征层压部件间的界面变异性；通过参数化研究分析相关长度与协方差核正则性对变异性的影响；使用近似贝叶斯计算方法从实验断裂数据中提取关键参数（相关长度）。

Result: 标准I型与II型断裂测试的参数化研究表明，相关长度是观测变异性的主要驱动因素，而协方差核的正则性影响甚微；近似贝叶斯计算能够有效从实验数据中提取相关长度。

Conclusion: 该框架为高保真虚拟测试和损伤容限复合材料机身设计中的不确定性预测管理提供了稳健途径，具备工业应用价值。

Abstract: To enable a numerical handling of uncertainties in composite structures, this work presents a stochastic finite-element framework aimed at improving the reliability assessment of aerospace composites, with particular attention to stiffener debonding. By representing interface variability between laminate parts with spatially correlated random fields, the method aims at considering scattering effect at a higher scale of simulation and testing. A parametric study carried out on standardized Mode I and Mode II fracture tests reveals that the correlation length is the primary driver of observed variability, while the regularity of the covariance kernel has only a marginal impact. To guarantee industrial relevance, we demonstrate that this key parameter can be extracted from experimental fracture data using an Approximate Bayesian Computation approach. The proposed methodology therefore offers a robust route to high-fidelity virtual testing and to the predictive management of uncertainties in the design of damage-tolerant composite airframes.

</details>


### [138] [Efficient Estimation of A-basis and B-Basis Value under Epistemic Uncertainty using Importance Sampling and Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13094)
*Elton Donfack-Siewe,Jérôme Morio,Sylvain Dubreuil,Jean-Philippe Navarro,Christian Fagiano*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在混合认知-偶然不确定性下，利用确定性数值模型估计保守设计分位数（如A-和B-basis值）的方法。该方法通过重要性采样和控制变量实现高效无偏估计，并利用Sobol灵敏度指标识别主要认知不确定性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空航天等安全关键领域需要保守分位数估计（如A-和B-basis）来保证可靠性，传统方法依赖实验数据，而实际中实验成本高昂、数据有限。作者旨在利用已验证的确定性数值模型替代实验，在混合不确定性下提供具有统计保证的保守设计分位数估计方法。

Method: 1) 将问题建模为混合偶然-认知不确定性框架，考虑材料数据有限、有限采样效应和代理模型误差；2) 采用重要性采样（Importance Sampling）和控制变量法（Control Variates）实现高效估计；3) 代理模型仅作为方差缩减工具，保证估计的无偏性和一致性；4) 在无额外计算成本下获得基于Sobol的灵敏度指标，识别主导认知不确定性源。

Result: 在结构模型上的数值实验验证了方法的可靠性和计算效率；大规模工业仿真应用证明了其在航空航天认证流程中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该方法在固定计算预算下能够提供准确、高效且具有统计保证的保守分位数估计，可作为航空认证中A-和B-basis估计的数值替代方案，并适用于实际工程环境。

Abstract: In aerospace certification and other safety-critical domains, conservative quantile estimation such as A- and B-basis values is essential to guarantee reliability. While these metrics are traditionally derived from experimental campaigns, this work focuses on their estimation using a validated deterministic numerical model. The problem is formulated under mixed aleatory-epistemic uncertainty, accounting for limited material data, finite sampling effects, and surrogate modeling errors. We propose a methodology for estimating conservative design quantiles with statistical guarantees under mixed uncertainties. The proposed method leverages importance sampling and control variates to achieve accurate and efficient estimates within a fixed computational budget. One key point is the surrogate model's role solely as a variance reduction device, which guarantees unbiased and consistent quantile estimation. By explicitly integrating all sources of uncertainty, the proposed framework provides a numerical alternative to estimate A-basis and B-Basis. Furthermore, Sobol-based sensitivity indices are obtained at no additional cost, offering insight into the dominant epistemic sources. Numerical experiments on structural models demonstrate the method's reliability and computational efficiency. In particular, the application to large-scale industrial simulations confirms its suitability for aerospace certification workflows and highlights its relevance for real world engineering environments.

</details>


### [139] [Scaling Demand-Side Flexibility Through Dynamic Tariffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13401)
*Lucas Brylle,Niels Andersen,Henrik Madsen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文论证了通过动态电价激励的隐性需求侧灵活性（DSF）是应对丹麦配电网电气化与可再生能源整合所带来挑战的最具可扩展性和成本效益的方案，可节省每座受限变电站1300-4800万丹麦克朗的电网投资。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丹麦配电网在电气化和可再生能源整合过程中面临备用容量不足、设备过载退化、电压不稳定及基础设施投资需求增加等严峻运营挑战，亟需可扩展且经济的解决方案。

Method: 基于实证消费数据（显示强价格响应行为）、丹麦市场模型3.0和电价模型3.0等监管框架分析，以及经济分析，论证隐性DSF机制相比显式灵活性方案的优势。

Result: 每座受限变电站通过延迟或避免电网加固可节省1300-4800万丹麦克朗，额外价值流包括避免峰值发电成本、减少并网延迟和降低停电风险。

Conclusion: 隐性DSF机制是实现收入中性、可扩展灵活性解决方案的必要路径，动态电价能够实时将电网容量约束信号传递给消费者，是实现这一目标的关键机制。

Abstract: The ongoing electrification and integration of renewable energy sources in Denmark's distribution grids pose significant operational challenges, including insufficient reserve capacity, component degradation due to overload, voltage instability, and increasing infrastructure investment requirements. This article argues that implicit demand-side flexibility (DSF) incentivized through dynamic tariffs offers the most scalable and cost-effective approach to address these challenges in a modern distribution network. We demonstrate that while explicit flexibility mechanisms provide operational certainty, they cannot scale to address system-wide congestion across heterogeneous customer bases. Drawing on empirical consumption data showing strong price-responsive behavior, varying prices due to, e.g., regulatory frameworks including the Danish Market Model 3.0 and Tariff Model 3.0, and economic analysis, we demonstrate potential grid savings of 13--48 million DKK per constrained substation through deferred or avoided reinforcement. We argue that implicit DSF mechanisms represent the necessary pathway for revenue-neutral scalable flexibility solutions that can defer costly grid reinforcements while maintaining system reliability. Beyond direct grid savings, additional value streams include avoided peak generation costs, reduced connection delays, and lower outage risk, further strengthening the economic case. Critically, dynamic tariffs offer the mechanism through which real-time grid constraints can be communicated to consumers, enabling price signals that accurately reflect the actual state of the capacity of the distribution network at any given point in time and space.

</details>
